   426	pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
   427	    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
   428	        Ok(service) => service,
   429	        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
   430	    };
   431	    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
   432	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   433	        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
   434	    };
   435	    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
   436	        None => false,
   437	        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
   438	        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
   439	    };
   440	    let boolean = |key: &str, default: bool| -> Result<bool, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   441	        match payload.get(key) {
   442	            None => Ok(default), Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => Ok(*value),
   443	            Some(_) => Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("{key} must be a boolean"))),
   444	        }
   445	    };
   446	    let (drain, allow_held) = match boolean("drain", true).and_then(|drain| Ok((drain, boolean("allowHeld", false)?))) {
   447	        Ok(options) => options,
   448	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   449	    };
   450	    if each_boundary && !drain { return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary requires drain:true"))})); }
   451	    let preserve_sessions = match payload.get("sessions").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
   452	        None if payload.get("sessions").is_none() => service.is_some(),
   453	        Some("preserve") if service.is_some() => true,
   454	        Some("expire") => false,
   455	        _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("sessions must be preserve (authenticated development or staging only) or expire"))})),
   456	    };
   457	    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
   458	    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
   459	        .and_then(|duration| if duration == "0ms" && drain { Ok(0) } else { crate::dev_clock::duration(duration) }) {
   460	        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
   461	        Err(error) => return respond(request, refusal_status(&error, 400), json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   462	    };
   463	    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
   464	    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
   465	    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
   466	    let deadline = Instant::now() + snapback4_core::engine::command::ADMISSION_TIMEOUT;
   467	    let _advance = loop {
   468	        if Instant::now() >= deadline {
   469	            let why = super::committer::queue_error(snapback4_core::engine::command::SubmitError::Full).refusal();
   470	            return respond(request, 503, json!({"denied":why}));
   471	        }
   472	        match advance.try_lock() {
   473	            Ok(guard) => break guard,
   474	            Err(std::sync::TryLockError::Poisoned(error)) => break error.into_inner(),
   475	            Err(std::sync::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => std::thread::park_timeout(Duration::from_millis(1)),
   476	        }
   477	    };
   478	    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
   479	    // @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000.000#9 — admitted clock work may outlive its acknowledgement; only the request owns the maintenance pause.
   480	    lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
   481	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
   482	    let facts = super::RequestFacts::capture(&request);
   483	    let advanced = shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
   484	        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(engine);
   485	        if let Some(principal) = &service {
   486	            if !shared.personas && engine.store().deployment_environment().map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
   487	            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&facts,shared,engine,principal)?;
   488	        } else { shared.dev_authority(engine)?; }
   489	        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
   490	        let floor = shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire);
   491	        let effective_before = clock.now().max(floor);
   492	        let mut next = clock.clone();
   493	        let now = (if milliseconds == 0 { next.now() } else { next.advance(milliseconds)? }).max(floor);
   494	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — refuse a saved boundary before any clock/session effect.
   495	        if drain && !allow_held && !shared.hold_schedules {
   496	            crate::dev_clock_store::refuse_held(engine, &before, clock.now(), now)?;
   497	        }
   498	        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary)?;
   499	        if !shared.personas {
   500	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — a retained disclosure floor is also the credential authority clock.
   501	            // A raw jump below that floor advances no business time and cannot
   502	            // extend credentials or leases. Selection must never see an older
   503	            // raw process time and revive an already expired family.
   504	            let effective_delta = now.checked_sub(effective_before)
   505	                .ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("effective clock advance overflow"))?;
   506	            let checkpoint = snapback4_core::metadata::StagingClock { now, offset: next.offset(), paused: true };
   507	            checkpoint_clock(engine, effective_delta, effective_before, preserve_sessions, &checkpoint).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
   508	        } else if service.is_some() {
   509	            let effective_delta = now.checked_sub(effective_before)
   510	                .ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("effective clock advance overflow"))?;
   511	            engine.advance_staging_clock_at(effective_delta, effective_before, preserve_sessions).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
   512	        } else if milliseconds > 0 {
   513	            engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
   514	        }
   515	        lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = true;
   516	        *clock = next;
   517	        let offset = clock.offset();
   518	        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
   519	        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(engine) {
   520	            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
   521	        } else { String::new() };
   522	        let lifetime = if shared.personas { "development offset lasts until restart" } else { "offset persists with this staging store across restarts" };
   523	        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (now includes the authoritative floor); {lifetime}{isolated}");
   524	        Ok((now, clock.now(), offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
   525	    });

     6	pub(super) struct SavedVersion { path: PathBuf, files: Vec<(PathBuf, Vec<u8>)>, complete: bool, refresh: bool }
     7	impl Drop for SavedVersion {
     8	    fn drop(&mut self) { if !self.complete && !self.refresh { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.path); } }

    10	impl SavedVersion {
    11	    pub(super) fn prepare(project: &Project, sources: &[(String,String)], bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound, options: &Options, deployment: &std::path::Path) -> Result<Option<Self>,String> {
    12	        let Some(name) = options.flags.get("save-version") else {return Ok(None);};
    13	        if sources.is_empty() || sources.iter().all(|(_,text)|text.trim().is_empty()) {return Err("not saved yet: a saved version needs nonempty Quarry source".into());}
    14	        if options.flags.contains_key("check") {return Err("--save-version captures a successful deployment; omit --check to save, or omit --save-version for a read-only preview".into());}
    15	        if project.root.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.starts_with(deployment.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?) {
    16	            return Err("cannot save a version inside deployment custody; run deploy from the application's source directory".into());
    17	        }
    18	        let suffix = name.strip_prefix("versions/v").ok_or("--save-version needs a new project-relative versions/vN directory")?;
    19	        if suffix.is_empty() || suffix.starts_with('0') || !suffix.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_digit()) {return Err("--save-version needs versions/vN with a positive numeric N".into());}
    20	        let parent=project.root.join("versions");
    21	        if std::fs::symlink_metadata(&parent).is_ok_and(|m|m.file_type().is_symlink()) {return Err("--save-version refuses a symlinked versions directory".into());}
    22	        std::fs::create_dir_all(&parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    23	        let path=project.root.join(name);
    24	        let refresh = match std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
    25	            Ok(meta) if meta.file_type().is_symlink() || !meta.is_dir() => return Err(format!("cannot refresh {name}: saved version must be a real directory")),
    26	            Ok(_) => {
    27	                let selected=sources.iter().cloned().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    28	                let existing=crate::client_compatibility::sources(&path).map_err(|e|format!("cannot refresh {name}: {e}"))?.into_iter().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    29	                if existing != selected {return Err(format!("cannot refresh {name}: selected source differs from the saved source; choose a new versions/vN"));}
    30	                true
    31	            }
    32	            Err(error) if error.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {std::fs::create_dir(&path).map_err(|e|format!("cannot save {name}: {e}"))?;false}
    33	            Err(error) => return Err(format!("cannot inspect {name}: {error}")),
    34	        };
    35	        let mut files = if refresh {Vec::new()} else {sources.iter().map(|(name,text)|(PathBuf::from(name),text.as_bytes().to_vec())).collect()};
    36	        files.push((PathBuf::from("generated/api.ts"),snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(bound).into_bytes()));
    37	        files.push((PathBuf::from("generated/package.json"),crate::project::GENERATED_PACKAGE.as_bytes().to_vec()));
    38	        let mut saved=Self {path,files,complete:false,refresh};
    39	        let fixture = match options.flags.get("schema").map(PathBuf::from) {
    40	            None=>project.root.join("fixtures"),
    41	            Some(source) if source.is_dir()=>source.join("fixtures"),
    42	            Some(source) if source.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="schema.q")=>source.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures"),
    43	            Some(source)=>source.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures",source.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())),
    44	        };
    45	        if fixture.is_dir() && !refresh {
    46	            let mut entries=std::fs::read_dir(&fixture).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    47	            entries.sort_by_key(|e|e.file_name());
    48	            for entry in entries {
    49	                if entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|e|e=="jsonl") || entry.file_name()=="test.sweep.json" {
    50	                    if !entry.file_type().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_file() {return Err("saved fixtures must be regular files".into());}
    51	                    saved.files.push((PathBuf::from("fixtures").join(entry.file_name()),std::fs::read(entry.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?));
    52	                }
    53	            }
    54	        }
    55	        if !refresh {saved.files.push((PathBuf::from("fixtures/.gitkeep"),Vec::new()));}
    56	        Ok(Some(saved))
    57	    }
    58	    pub(super) fn finish(&mut self, release: u64, source_hash: &str) -> Result<(),String> {
    59	        self.files.push((PathBuf::from("release.json"),format!("{}\n",serde_json::json!({"release":release,"sourceHash":source_hash,"cliVersion":env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")})).into_bytes()));
    60	        for (name,bytes) in &self.files {
    61	            let path=self.path.join(name);
    62	            std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    63	            if self.refresh {
    64	                let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("refresh-{}",std::process::id()));
    65	                std::fs::write(&temporary,bytes).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    66	                std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    67	            } else {
    68	                use std::io::Write;
    69	                let mut file=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    70	                file.write_all(bytes).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    71	            }
    72	        }
    73	        self.complete=true;
    74	        eprintln!("{} release {release} sources, generated client, contemporary fixtures and release.json at {}",if self.refresh {"refreshed"} else {"saved"},self.path.display());
    75	        Ok(())
    76	    }

     7	/// Conservative input-domain inclusion: every value accepted by old is accepted by new.
     8	pub fn accepts(old: &A, new: &A) -> bool {
     9	    accepts_in(old,new,None,None)
    10	}
    11	
    12	fn accepts_in(old: &A, new: &A, old_bound: Option<&Bound>, new_bound: Option<&Bound>) -> bool {
    13	    if old==new {return true;}
    14	    match (old,new) {
    15	        (A::Optional(a),A::Optional(b))=>accepts_in(a,b,old_bound,new_bound),
    16	        (A::Optional(_),_)=>false,
    17	        (a,A::Optional(b))=>accepts_in(a,b,old_bound,new_bound),
    18	        (A::Ref{table:old},A::Ref{table:new})=>new_bound.is_some_and(|bound|bound.schema.table_renames.get(new)==Some(old))
    19	            && old_bound.is_some_and(|bound|bound.schema.table(old).is_some()),
    20	        (A::Text{min:a,max:b,format:af},A::Text{min:c,max:d,format:bf})=>c<=a && d>=b && (bf.is_none() || af==bf),
    21	        (A::IntRange{..},A::Int)=>true,
    22	        (A::IntRange{min:a,max:b},A::IntRange{min:c,max:d})=>c<=a && d>=b,
    23	        (A::Enum{variants:a},A::Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),
    24	        (A::Bytes{max:a},A::Bytes{max:b}) | (A::Image{max:a},A::Image{max:b}) |
    25	        (A::Video{max:a},A::Video{max:b})=>b>=a,
    26	        (A::Json{max_bytes:a},A::Json{max_bytes:b})=>b>=a,
    27	        (A::File{max:a,types:at},A::File{max:b,types:bt})=>b>=a && snapback4_core::assets::FILE_TYPES.iter().all(|t|!snapback4_core::assets::file_accepts(at,t)||snapback4_core::assets::file_accepts(bt,t)),
    28	        (A::List{..}|A::ListRange{..},A::List{..}|A::ListRange{..})=> {
    29	            let parts=|t:&A|match t {A::List{of,max}=>(0,*max,of.clone()),A::ListRange{of,min,max}=>(*min,*max,of.clone()),_=>unreachable!()};

    37	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    38	enum ResultChange { Same, Additive, Changed }
    39	
    40	fn combine(fields: impl IntoIterator<Item=ResultChange>, extra: bool) -> ResultChange {
    41	    let mut additive=extra;
    42	    for field in fields {
    43	        match field {ResultChange::Changed=>return ResultChange::Changed,ResultChange::Additive=>additive=true,ResultChange::Same=>{}}
    44	    }
    45	    if additive {ResultChange::Additive} else {ResultChange::Same}
    46	}

    48	fn table_fields(name: &str, bound: &Bound) -> Option<BTreeMap<String,String>> {
    49	    let schema=bound.schema.public_projection();
    50	    let table=schema.tables.get(name)?;
    51	    Some(table.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|!table.engine_field(n)).map(|(n,t)|(n.clone(),format!("{t:?};private={};constraints={:?}",table.read_optional(n),table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..} if field==n)).collect::<Vec<_>>()))).collect())
    52	}
    53	
    54	fn map_change<T>(old: &BTreeMap<String,T>, new: &BTreeMap<String,T>, compare: impl Fn(&T,&T)->ResultChange) -> ResultChange {
    55	    combine(old.iter().map(|(name,old)|new.get(name).map(|new|compare(old,new)).unwrap_or(ResultChange::Changed)),new.len()>old.len())
    56	}
    57	
    58	fn result_change(old: &Ty, new: &Ty, old_bound: &Bound, new_bound: &Bound) -> ResultChange {
    59	    if result(old,old_bound)==result(new,new_bound) {return ResultChange::Same;}
    60	    match (old,new) {
    61	        (Ty::Optional(a),Ty::Optional(b))|(Ty::Omittable(a),Ty::Omittable(b))
    62	        |(Ty::List(a,_),Ty::List(b,_))|(Ty::Collection(a,_),Ty::Collection(b,_))=>result_change(a,b,old_bound,new_bound),
    63	        (Ty::Object(a)|Ty::RowShape(a)|Ty::RowObject(a),Ty::Object(b)|Ty::RowShape(b)|Ty::RowObject(b))=>map_change(a,b,|a,b|result_change(a,b,old_bound,new_bound)),
    64	        (Ty::Row(a)|Ty::WriteRow(a)|Ty::Page(a,_),Ty::Row(b)|Ty::WriteRow(b)|Ty::Page(b,_)) if a==b=>{
    65	            let (Some(a),Some(b))=(table_fields(a,old_bound),table_fields(b,new_bound)) else{return ResultChange::Changed;};
    66	            combine(a.iter().map(|(name,kind)|if b.get(name)==Some(kind){ResultChange::Same}else{ResultChange::Changed}),b.len()>a.len())
    67	        }
    68	        _=>ResultChange::Changed,
    69	    }
    70	}

    72	fn action_result_change(old: Option<&BTreeMap<String,A>>, new: Option<&BTreeMap<String,A>>) -> ResultChange {
    73	    match (old,new) {
    74	        (None,None)=>ResultChange::Same,
    75	        (Some(old),Some(new))=>combine(old.iter().map(|(name,kind)|if new.get(name)==Some(kind){ResultChange::Same}else{ResultChange::Changed}),new.len()>old.len()),
    76	        _=>ResultChange::Changed,
    77	    }
    78	}

    82	fn result(ty: &Ty, bound: &Bound) -> String {

    84	        Ty::Row(name)|Ty::WriteRow(name)|Ty::Page(name,_)=> {

    88	                format!("{}:{name}:{fields:?}",if matches!(ty,Ty::Page(..)){"page"}else{"row"})
    89	            } else {format!("{ty:?}")}
    90	        }

    95	        Ty::Text(max)=>format!("text<={max}"),

   100	fn refusals(p: &BoundProgram) -> Vec<String> {
   101	    fn walk(v: &serde_json::Value, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
   102	        match v {
   103	            serde_json::Value::Object(o)=> {
   104	                if let Some(r)=o.get("Require") {out.push(format!("{}:{}",r["code"],r["message"]));}
   105	                for v in o.values(){walk(v,out);}
   106	            }
   107	            serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{walk(v,out);},
   108	            _=>{}
   109	        }
   110	    }
   111	    let mut out=Vec::new();walk(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program.body).unwrap(),&mut out);out
   112	}
   113	
   114	/// Reports are advisory: compatibility cannot license calls or prove unchanged behavior.
   115	pub fn compare(old: &Bound, new: &Bound, label: &str) -> Vec<String> {
   116	    let mut lines=Vec::new();
   117	    for before in old.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Job) {
   118	        let name=&before.program.name;
   119	        let prefix=format!("old-client compatibility ({label}), operation {name}");
   120	        let Some(after)=new.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*name) else {
   121	            lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed operation; old calls get E_OP; keep `alias {name} = replacement` for a same-module query/mutation, or retain a wrapper"));continue;
   122	        };
   123	        if before.program.kind!=after.program.kind {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: operation kind changed; retain the old endpoint kind"));continue;}
   124	        let compatible_rename = |new_name: &str, new_kind: &A| before.program.args.iter().find(|(old_name, old_kind)|
   125	            !after.program.args.contains_key(*old_name) && accepts_in(old_kind,new_kind,Some(old),Some(new)) &&
   126	            after.program.arg_aliases.get(*old_name).is_none_or(|canonical|canonical==new_name));
   127	        for (arg,kind) in &after.program.args {
   128	            if before.program.args.contains_key(arg) { continue; }
   129	            if let Some((old_arg,old_kind))=before.program.args.iter().find(|(old,_)|after.program.arg_aliases.get(*old)==Some(arg)) {
   130	                if !accepts_in(old_kind,kind,Some(old),Some(new)) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: aliased argument {old_arg} → {arg} narrows the old input domain ({old_kind:?} → {kind:?}); retain the old input domain or a compatible wrapper"));}
   131	            } else if !matches!(kind,A::Optional(_)) {
   132	                if let Some((old,_))=compatible_rename(arg,kind) {
   133	                    lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: required argument {old} appears renamed to {arg}; old calls get E_INPUT; declare `{arg}: <new type> alias {old}` if it is the same value, or retain a compatible wrapper"));
   134	                } else {
   135	                    lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: clients calling without new required argument {arg} get E_INPUT; give {arg} a fitting `default <literal>` or retain a compatible wrapper"));
   136	                }
   137	            }
   138	        }
   139	        for (arg,kind) in &before.program.args {
   140	            match after.program.args.get(arg) {
   141	                None if after.program.arg_aliases.contains_key(arg)=>{
   142	                    let next=&after.program.args[&after.program.arg_aliases[arg]];
   143	                    if !accepts_in(kind,next,Some(old),Some(new)){lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: aliased argument {arg} retyped or narrowed ({kind:?} → {next:?}); retain its old input domain"));}
   144	                },
   145	                None if after.program.args.iter().any(|(new,next)|compatible_rename(new,next).is_some_and(|(old,_)|old==arg))=>{},
   146	                None=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed argument {arg}; old calls sending it get E_INPUT; retain `{arg}: <old type> deprecated` on a query/mutation")),
   147	                Some(next) if !accepts_in(kind,next,Some(old),Some(new))=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: argument {arg} retyped or narrowed ({kind:?} → {next:?}); old values may get E_INPUT; retain the old input domain or a compatible wrapper")),
   148	                _=>{}
   149	            }
   150	        }
   151	        for (alias,canonical) in &before.program.arg_aliases {
   152	            let old_kind=&before.program.args[canonical];
   153	            let next=after.program.args.get(alias).or_else(||after.program.arg_aliases.get(alias).and_then(|name|after.program.args.get(name)));
   154	            match next {
   155	                None=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed argument alias {alias}; old calls sending it get E_INPUT; retain its alias or a compatible wrapper")),
   156	                Some(kind) if !accepts_in(old_kind,kind,Some(old),Some(new))=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: argument alias {alias} retyped or narrowed ({old_kind:?} → {kind:?}); retain its old input domain")),
   157	                _=>{}
   158	            }
   159	        }
   160	        let mut positions:BTreeMap<String,String>=BTreeMap::new();
   161	        for canonical in before.program.args.keys() {
   162	            for spelling in std::iter::once(canonical).chain(before.program.arg_aliases.iter().filter_map(|(alias,target)|(target==canonical).then_some(alias))) {
   163	                let target=after.program.args.contains_key(spelling).then_some(spelling).or_else(||after.program.arg_aliases.get(spelling));
   164	                if let Some(target)=target {
   165	                    if let Some(prior)=positions.insert(target.clone(),canonical.clone())&&prior!=*canonical {
   166	                        lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: independent arguments {prior} and {canonical} now share {target}; old calls containing both positions get E_INPUT; retain separate arguments or a compatible wrapper"));
   167	                    }
   168	                }
   169	            }
   170	        }
   171	        for (table,before_table) in old.schema.visible_tables() {
   172	            let Some(after_table)=new.schema.table(table) else { continue; };
   173	            if !snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&before.program).iter().any(|site|site.table==*table)
   174	                && !snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&before.program).iter().any(|(name,_)|name==table) { continue; }
   175	            for field in before_table.columns.keys().filter(|f|!before_table.read_optional(f)&&after_table.read_optional(f)) {
   176	                lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: {table}.{field} is now audience-optional; handle an omitted row property or a null explicit scalar before adopting the new column read rule"));
   177	            }
   178	        }
   179	        let action_change=action_result_change(old.schema.action_specs.get(name).and_then(|s|s.returns.as_ref()),new.schema.action_specs.get(name).and_then(|s|s.returns.as_ref()));
   180	        let typed_change=result_change(&before.result,&after.result,old,new);
   181	        let change=combine([action_change,typed_change],false);
   182	        match change {
   183	            ResultChange::Additive=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT NOTE: result shape added fields while preserving prior fields and domains; old clients may ignore the additions")),
   184	            ResultChange::Changed=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: result shape/value domain changed; preserve the old projection/domain or introduce a new operation before retiring old clients")),
   185	            ResultChange::Same=>{},
   186	        }
   187	        let old_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(before,old);
   188	        let new_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(after,new);
   189	        // A domain introduced only for a newly returned field is covered by
   190	        // the additive result note. Existing or removed domains still need review.
   191	        let changed:Vec<_>=old_domains.keys().collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().filter(|key|old_domains.get(*key)!=new_domains.get(*key)).cloned().collect();
   192	        if !changed.is_empty() {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: declared source domains consumed by this operation changed: {}; results or refusals may change even when the TypeScript type stays number/string; preserve the old domains or verify old-client handling",changed.join(", ")));}
   193	        if refusals(before)!=refusals(after) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: BEHAVIOR REVIEW: refusal codes/messages changed ({:?} → {:?}); verify intended denials separately from input breaks",refusals(before),refusals(after)));}
   194	    }
   195	    lines
   196	}
   197	
   198	/// Human result advice groups source columns across operations. JSON keeps compare().
   199	pub fn compact(lines:&[String])->Vec<String>{
   200	    use std::collections::BTreeSet;
   201	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeSet<String>>::new();
   202	    for line in lines {
   203	        let Some((prefix,detail))=line.split_once(": RESULT REVIEW: ") else {out.push(line.clone());continue};
   204	        let operation=prefix.rsplit_once("operation ").map(|(_,name)|name).unwrap_or("unknown");
   205	        if let Some((_,columns))=detail.split_once("declared source domains consumed by this operation changed: ") {
   206	            for column in columns.split(';').next().unwrap_or(columns).split(", "){groups.entry(column.into()).or_default().insert(operation.into());}
   207	        }else{groups.entry(detail.into()).or_default().insert(operation.into());}
   208	    }
   209	    for(detail,ops)in groups{out.push(format!("old-client compatibility: RESULT REVIEW: {detail}; operations [{}]; verify old-client handling (full per-operation evidence in --json)",ops.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));}
   210	    out
   211	}

     1	//! The CLI's local service-seat door. This capability never comes from a bearer session.
     2	use super::{Shared, single_header, authority_host, respond_with_cors};

    10	mod protocol;
    11	use protocol::{Mint, CLIENT, SERVER, PROOF};
    12	pub(crate) use protocol::Authority;
    13	const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4096;
    14	const SECRET_LEN: usize = 64;

    16	fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    17	    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    18	    path.push("-token");
    19	    path.into()
    20	}

    22	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
    23	pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    24	    #[cfg(unix)] {
    25	        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
    26	        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
    27	        file.set_len(0)?;
    28	        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
    29	        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
    30	        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    31	    }
    32	    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
    33	        let _ = store;
    34	        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    35	    }
    36	}
    37	
    38	fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    39	    #[cfg(unix)] {
    40	        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    41	        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
    42	        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
    43	            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
    44	        }
    45	        let mut secret = String::new();
    46	        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
    47	        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
    48	            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
    49	        }
    50	        Ok(secret)
    51	    }
    52	    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
    53	        let _ = store;
    54	        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    55	    }
    56	}

    58	fn refusal(code: &str, family: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Refusal {
    59	    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
    60	}

    62	pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    63	    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
    64	        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
    65	        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
    66	}

    68	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
    69	pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    70	    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
    71	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
    72	            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
    73	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
    74	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    75	    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
    76	        return denied(request);
    77	    };
    78	    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
    79	        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    80	    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
    81	        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
    82	        _ => return denied(request),
    83	    };
    84	    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
    85	        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
    86	        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
    87	            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
    88	        },
    89	        _ => return denied(request),
    90	    };
    91	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    92	    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
    93	        return denied(request);
    94	    }
    95	    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
    96	        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
    97	        _ => return denied(request),
    98	    };
    99	    let binding = mint.binding();
   100	    let Some(server) = server else {
   101	        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
   102	        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
   103	            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
   104	    };
   105	    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
   106	        return denied(request);
   107	    }
   108	    let result = shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
   109	        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
   110	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — live mint and resolution use the same host clock.
   111	            let now = shared.now();
   112	            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, now, mint.days)
   113	        })
   114	    });
   115	    let reply = match result {
   116	        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
   117	        Err(error) => {
   118	            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
   119	                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
   120	            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
   121	            json!({"denied": why})
   122	        }
   123	    };
   124	    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
   125	        return denied(request);
   126	    };
   127	    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
   128	}
   129	
   130	fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
   131	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
   132	    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
   133	    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
   134	    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
   135	    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
   136	    let mut response = String::new();
   137	    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
   138	    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
   139	        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
   140	        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
   141	        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
   142	}
   143	
   144	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
   145	pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
   146	    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
   147	    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
   148	    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
   149	    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
   150	    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
   151	    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
   152	        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
   153	    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
   154	    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
   155	    let binding = mint.binding();
   156	    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
   157	    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
   158	    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
   159	    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
   160	        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
   161	    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
   162	        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
   163	    }
   164	    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
   165	    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
   166	        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
   167	    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
   168	        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
   169	    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
   170	        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
   171	        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
   172	    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
   173	        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
   174	            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
   175	        }
   176	        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
   177	    }
   178	    let session = &reply["session"];
   179	    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
   180	    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
   181	    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
   182	        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));

    43	/// Shared schema-first ordering retains saved modules' original namespaces.
    44	pub(crate) fn sources(path: &std::path::Path) -> io::Result<Vec<(String, String)>> {
    45	    if !path.try_exists()? {return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::NotFound,format!("{}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist",path.display())));}
    46	    let bad = |message: &str| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, message);
    47	    let mut paths = if path.is_dir() {
    48	        std::fs::read_dir(path)?.map(|entry| entry.map(|entry| entry.path())).collect::<io::Result<Vec<_>>>()?
    49	            .into_iter().filter(|path| path.is_file() && path.extension().is_some_and(|extension| extension == "q")).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    50	    } else { vec![path.to_owned()] };
    51	    paths.sort_by_key(|path| (path.file_name().is_none_or(|name| name != "schema.q"), path.clone()));
    52	    if paths.is_empty() { return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::NotFound, "not saved yet: saved source directory has no Quarry .q sources")); }
    53	    let sources: Vec<(String, String)> = paths.into_iter().map(|path| {
    54	        let name = path.file_name().and_then(|name| name.to_str()).filter(|name| name.ends_with(".q"))
    55	            .ok_or_else(|| bad("saved sources need their original UTF-8 .q file names"))?;
    56	        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| io::Error::new(e.kind(), format!("saved source {}: {}{e}", path.display(), if e.kind()==io::ErrorKind::NotFound {"not saved yet: "}else{""})))?;

//! A bounded historical asset list becomes one receipt-owned child per element.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — stable element identities and current values round-trip without copying bytes or acquiring another home.
use super::*;
use crate::error::MeterKind;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) fn item_type(kind: &ColumnType) -> Option<&ColumnType> {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::Optional(kind) => item_type(kind),
        ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. } => Some(of),
        _ => None,
    }
}
fn setup(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_each_rows(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,child_id TEXT NOT NULL,asset_id TEXT NOT NULL,position INTEGER NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,child_t,child_id),UNIQUE(definition,parent_t,parent_id,asset_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    Ok(())
}

     5	    pub(super) fn parse_args(&mut self) -> Fallible<Vec<ArgDecl>> {
     6	        self.take("(")?;
     7	        let mut args = Vec::new();
     8	        while !self.at(")") {
     9	            let name = self.peek().clone();
    10	            let binding = self.local_name()?;
    11	            self.take(":")?;
    12	            let type_site = self.peek().site.clone();
    13	            let type_ = self.parse_type(false).map_err(|mut d| {
    14	                if d.mechanical && d.message.starts_with("once is a column modifier") {
    15	                    // Nested element types need a whole-list rewrite, not an argument fragment.
    16	                    if d.site == type_site { d.site = name.site.clone(); d.rewrite = format!("{binding}: {}", d.rewrite); }
    17	                    else { d.mechanical = false; }
    18	                }

    23	            let mut deprecated = false;

    25	                if self.maybe("default") {
    26	                    if default.is_some() { return Err(syntax(&self.peek().site, "an argument has at most one default")); }
    27	                    default = Some(self.expression(0, &[",", ")", "alias", "deprecated"])?);
    28	                } else if self.maybe("alias") {
    29	                    if alias.is_some() { return Err(syntax(&self.peek().site, "an argument has at most one alias")); }
    30	                    alias = Some(self.local_name()?);
    31	                } else if self.maybe("deprecated") {
    32	                    if deprecated { return Err(syntax(&self.peek().site, "an argument is marked deprecated at most once")); }
    33	                    deprecated = true;
    34	                } else { break; }
    35	            }

    38	            if !self.maybe(",") {
    39	                break;
    40	            }
    41	        }
    42	        self.take(")")?;
    43	        Ok(args)

    35	pub(super) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,maps:&Maps)->Schema {
    36	    for(name,map)in maps {if let Some(t)=schema.tables.get_mut(name){table(t,map);}}
    37	    for e in &mut schema.extractions {
    38	        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.child){e.reference=map.get(&e.reference).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||e.reference.clone());e.fields=std::mem::take(&mut e.fields).into_iter().map(|(dest,source)|(map.get(&dest).cloned().unwrap_or(dest),source)).collect();}
    39	        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.parent){for source in e.fields.values_mut(){if let Some(new)=map.get(source){*source=new.clone();}}}
    40	    }schema
    41	}
    42	pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&BTreeMap<String,i64>,renamed_tables:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Maps,RuntimeError>{
    43	    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    44	    if !exists && next.asset_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(Maps::new())}
    45	    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _asset_column_renames(t INTEGER NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,origin TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    46	    let mut maps=Maps::new();
    47	    for (name,current) in &next.tables {
    48	        let Some(t)=catalog.get(name) else {continue};
    49	        let mut aliases:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    50	        for (new,old) in next.asset_renames.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
    51	            if old==new || current.columns.contains_key(old) || !current.columns.get(new).is_some_and(asset){return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} requires one predecessor-only source and one asset destination")))}
    52	            let root=aliases.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
    53	            if aliases.get(new).is_some_and(|r|r!=&root){return Err(refusal("asset rename cannot merge independent column lifetimes"))}
    54	            for c in [old,new] {aliases.insert(c.clone(),root.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _asset_column_renames VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,c,root])?;}
    55	        }
    56	        let mut selected=BTreeMap::new();for c in current.columns.keys(){if let Some(root)=aliases.get(c){if selected.insert(root.clone(),c.clone()).is_some(){return Err(refusal("two names for the same asset column cannot be active together"))}}}
    57	        let map:BTreeMap<_,_>=aliases.into_iter().filter_map(|(old,root)|selected.get(&root).filter(|new|**new!=old).map(|new|(old,new.clone()))).collect();
    58	        for(old,new)in &map {
    59	            let prior:Option<String>=conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
    60	            if let Some(prior)=prior {
    61	                let prior:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&prior).map_err(|e|refusal(e.to_string()))?;
    62	                let prior=prior.with_table_names(renamed_tables)?;
    63	                if let Some(before)=prior.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(old)){
    64	                    let source=&before.columns[old];let destination=&current.columns[new];
    65	                    if reference(source)||reference(destination){
    66	                        if source!=destination||before.on_delete.get(old)!=current.on_delete.get(new)||before.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*old)||current.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*new){return Err(refusal("reference rename requires identical nominal type, deletion policy and uncaptured ownership in both directions"))}
    67	                    }
    68	                }
    69	            }
    70	            for rows in ["r","_dormant_rows","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
    71	                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
    72	                let a=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(old).unwrap());let b=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(new).unwrap());
    73	                let conflict:bool=conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {rows} WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL AND json_type(data,?3) IS NOT NULL AND json_extract(data,?2)!=json_extract(data,?3))"),params![t,a,b],|r|r.get(0))?;
    74	                if conflict{return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} has a conflicting physical destination")))}
    75	                conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET data=json_remove(json_set(data,?3,json_extract(data,?2)),?2) WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL"),params![t,a,b])?;
    76	            }
    77	            conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_column=?3 WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_column=?2",params![name,old,new])?;
    78	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_refs")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
    79	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_values")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
    80	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_columns")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_columns SET column_name=?3 WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![name,old,new])?;}
    81	        }
    82	        if !map.is_empty(){
    83	            for rows in ["_dormant_narrow_rows","_dormant_tables"] {
    84	                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
    85	                let condition=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{"table_name=?1"}else{"t=?1"};
    86	                let value=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{rusqlite::types::Value::Text(name.clone())}else{rusqlite::types::Value::Integer(*t)};
    87	                let definitions:Vec<String>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT DISTINCT definition FROM {rows} WHERE {condition}"))?.query_map([&value],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    88	                for text in definitions {let mut definition:Table=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;table(&mut definition,&map);conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET definition=?2 WHERE {condition} AND definition=?3"),params![value,canonical_json(&definition),text])?;}
    89	            }
    90	            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
    91	        }
    92	    }
    93	    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    94	    Ok(maps)
    95	}

    12	    if !names.insert((d.table.clone(),d.name.clone())) { diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,format!("duplicate {word} name on this table"),"give each historical transform its own name")); return; }
    13	    let decl = ast::ProgramDecl { delivery: None, held:false, predicts:false, server:false, kind:ast::ProgramKind::Query,
    14	        name: format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},d.table,d.name), args:d.args.clone(), prelude:vec![],
    15	        body:vec![ast::Stmt::Return { value:d.value.clone(), site:d.site.clone() }], native:false, site:d.site.clone() };
    16	    let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind_historical(catalog,schema,"schema",&decl,diagnostics) else {return};
    17	    let Ty::Object(fields) = &bound.result else { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("{word} returns an object patch"),"{ column: historicalValue }")); return; };
    18	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&d.table) else {diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,format!("{word} table is not declared"),"declare the current table"));return};
    19	    for (c,ty) in fields {
    20	        if !down {if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(c) {
    21	            if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::from_column(kind)) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfill {}.{c} returns {}, expected {}",d.table,ty.name(),kind),"return a value fitting the candidate column")); }
    22	        }}
    23	        if !outputs.insert((d.table.clone(),c.clone())) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("{word}s overlap on {}.{c}",d.table),"combine simultaneous outputs in one declaration")); }
    24	    }
    25	    let fill = Backfill { table:d.table.clone(), name:d.name.clone(), inputs:d.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),scalar_to_column(&a.type_))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(), outputs:fields.keys().cloned().collect(), program:bound.program };
    26	    let admitted=if down {fill.admit_down(schema)}else{fill.admit(schema)};
    27	    if let Err(why) = admitted { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,why,"use deterministic typed fields and a scalar patch").guide("deploy")); }
    28	    if down {schema.downfills.push(fill.normalized())}else{schema.backfills.push(fill.normalized())}
    29	}
    30	
    31	pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    32	    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.extractions) {
    33	        let extraction=snapback4_core::schema::Extraction{each:d.each,child:d.child.clone(),parent:d.parent.clone(),reference:d.reference.clone(),fields:d.fields.iter().cloned().collect()};
    34	        schema.extractions.push(extraction);
    35	    }
    36	    let mut forward_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut forward_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    37	    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.backfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);}
    38	    let mut down_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut down_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    39	    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);}
    40	    for table in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
    41	        if let Some(old)=&table.renamed_from {
    42	            if old==&table.name || catalog.tables.contains_key(old) || schema.table_renames.values().any(|v|v==old) {
    43	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&table.site,"table rename requires one distinct predecessor-only source","keep one name for each row lifetime"));
    44	            } else {schema.table_renames.insert(table.name.clone(),old.clone());}
    45	        }
    46	        for column in table.columns.iter().filter(|c|c.renamed_from.is_some()) {
    47	            let old=column.renamed_from.as_ref().unwrap();
    48	            if old==&column.name || table.columns.iter().any(|c|&c.name==old) {
    49	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("rename source {}.{old} must be a distinct predecessor-only column",table.name),"remove the current old column and retain only the renamed declaration"));continue
    50	            }
    51	            fn asset(kind:&ast::TypeKind)->bool {match kind {ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}=>true,ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>asset(&item.kind),_=>false}}
    52	            if (asset(&column.type_.kind)||matches!(&column.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref{..})) && column.maintained.is_none() && column.sequence.is_none() && column.written_by.is_none() {
    53	                schema.asset_renames.entry(table.name.clone()).or_default().insert(column.name.clone(),old.clone());continue;
    54	            }
    55	            if matches!(&column.type_.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref{..}|ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}|ast::TypeKind::List{..})
    56	                || column.maintained.is_some() || column.sequence.is_some() || column.written_by.is_some()
    57	            {
    58	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("column rename on {}.{} requires an authoritative scalar without reference, asset, collection, captured or lifetime ownership",table.name,column.name),"use a bridge migration that preserves the protected field's authority and lifetime"));continue
    59	            }
    60	            let declaration=|input:&str,output:&str,prefix:&str|ast::BackfillDecl{
    61	                table:table.name.clone(),name:format!("$rename:{prefix}:{old}:{}",column.name),
    62	                args:vec![ast::ArgDecl{alias:None,default:None,deprecated:false,name:input.into(),type_:column.type_.clone(),site:column.site.clone()}],
    63	                value:ast::Expr::Object{fields:vec![ast::ObjectField{spread:false,name:output.into(),value:Some(ast::Expr::Name{name:input.into(),site:column.site.clone()}),site:column.site.clone()}],site:column.site.clone()},site:column.site.clone()
    64	            };
    65	            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(old,&column.name,"up"),false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);
    66	            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(&column.name,old,"down"),true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	}

     1	//! SQLite erases booleans to integers. Recover direct column types from the
     2	//! adopted schema and SQL projection, never by guessing from names or 0/1.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.26 — positional JSON stays compatible; boolean recovery is presentation on the operator's adopted snapshot.
     4	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — direct schema-proven media cells use their ordinary wire JSON in object rows.
     5	use snapback4_core::{inspection::InspectionColumnKind as Kind, schema::{ColumnType, Schema}};
     6	use sqlparser::{ast::{Expr, ObjectName, Query, SelectItem, SelectItemQualifiedWildcardKind, SetExpr, Statement, TableFactor}, dialect::SQLiteDialect, parser::Parser};
     7	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     8	
     9	type Columns = Vec<(String, Kind)>;
    10	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, Columns>;
    11	
    12	pub(super) fn kinds(sql: &str, schema: &Schema, count: usize, retained: bool) -> Result<Vec<Kind>, String> {
    13	    let mut tables: Tables = schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| {
    14	        let mut columns = vec![("id".into(), Kind::Plain)];
    15	        columns.extend(table.columns.iter().filter(|(name, _)| *name != "id")
    16	            .map(|(name, kind)| (name.to_ascii_lowercase(), if matches!(kind.required(), ColumnType::Bool) { Kind::Bool } else if kind.contains_asset() { Kind::Media } else { Kind::Plain })));
    17	        if retained {columns.extend([("_snapback_state".into(),Kind::Plain),("_snapback_source".into(),Kind::Plain),("_snapback_columns".into(),Kind::Plain)]);}
    18	        (name.to_ascii_lowercase(), columns)
    19	    }).collect();
    20	    let statements = Parser::parse_sql(&SQLiteDialect {}, sql).map_err(|e| format!("--json-objects SQL projection: {e}; use --json for SQLite-specific syntax"))?;
    21	    let [Statement::Query(query)] = statements.as_slice() else { return Err("--json-objects takes a SELECT".into()); };
    22	    let columns = query_columns(query, &mut tables)?;
    23	    if columns.len() != count { return Err("--json-objects cannot determine this projection's column types; select explicit columns or use --json".into()); }
    24	    Ok(columns.into_iter().map(|(_, kind)| kind).collect())
    25	}
    26	
    27	fn name(name: &ObjectName) -> Option<String> {
    28	    // SQLite schema qualification bypasses CTE shadowing; do not erase it.
    29	    if name.0.len() != 1 { return None; }
    30	    name.0.last()?.as_ident().map(|id| id.value.to_ascii_lowercase())
    31	}
    32	
    33	fn query_columns(query: &Query, inherited: &mut Tables) -> Result<Columns, String> {
    34	    let mut tables = inherited.clone();
    35	    if let Some(with) = &query.with {
    36	        if with.recursive { return Err("--json-objects does not infer recursive CTE types; use --json".into()); }
    37	        for cte in &with.cte_tables {
    38	            if tables.contains_key(&cte.alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()) {
    39	                return Err("--json-objects does not infer shadowing CTEs; use --json".into());
    40	            }
    41	            let mut columns = query_columns(&cte.query, &mut tables)?;
    42	            for (column, alias) in columns.iter_mut().zip(&cte.alias.columns) { column.0 = alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase(); }
    43	            tables.insert(cte.alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase(), columns);
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	    set_columns(&query.body, &mut tables)
    47	}
    48	
    49	fn relation(factor: &TableFactor, tables: &mut Tables) -> Result<(String, Columns), String> {
    50	    match factor {
    51	        TableFactor::Table { name: object, alias, args: None, .. } => {
    52	            let key = name(object).ok_or("--json-objects does not infer schema-qualified relations; use --json")?;
    53	            let columns = tables.get(&key).ok_or("--json-objects needs application tables or typed CTEs")?.clone();
    54	            Ok((alias.as_ref().map(|a| a.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()).unwrap_or(key), columns))
    55	        }
    56	        TableFactor::Derived { subquery, alias, .. } => Ok((alias.as_ref().map(|a| a.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()).unwrap_or_default(), query_columns(subquery, tables)?)),
    57	        _ => Err("--json-objects cannot infer this table expression; use --json".into()),
    58	    }
    59	}
    60	
    61	fn set_columns(body: &SetExpr, tables: &mut Tables) -> Result<Columns, String> {
    62	    match body {
    63	        SetExpr::Query(q) => query_columns(q, tables),
    64	        SetExpr::SetOperation { left, right, .. } => {
    65	            let mut left = set_columns(left, tables)?;
    66	            let right = set_columns(right, tables)?;
    67	            if left.len() != right.len() { return Err("incompatible SQL projections".into()); }
    68	            for (l, r) in left.iter_mut().zip(right) { if l.1 != r.1 { l.1 = Kind::Plain; } }
    69	            Ok(left)
    70	        }
    71	        SetExpr::Select(select) => {
    72	            let mut sources = Vec::new();
    73	            for from in &select.from {
    74	                sources.push(relation(&from.relation, tables)?);
    75	                for join in &from.joins { sources.push(relation(&join.relation, tables)?); }
    76	            }
    77	            let mut output = Vec::new();
    78	            for item in &select.projection {
    79	                match item {
    80	                    SelectItem::Wildcard(_) => output.extend(sources.iter().flat_map(|(_, cols)| cols.clone())),
    81	                    SelectItem::QualifiedWildcard(SelectItemQualifiedWildcardKind::ObjectName(object), _) => {
    82	                        let key = name(object).ok_or("unsupported wildcard")?;
    83	                        let (_, cols) = sources.iter().find(|(alias, _)| *alias == key).ok_or("unknown wildcard table")?;
    84	                        output.extend(cols.clone());
    85	                    }
    86	                    SelectItem::UnnamedExpr(expr) => {
    87	                        let mut direct = expr;
    88	                        while let Expr::Nested(inner) = direct { direct = inner; }
    89	                        let label = match direct {
    90	                            Expr::Identifier(id) => id.value.clone(),
    91	                            Expr::CompoundIdentifier(ids) => ids.last().unwrap().value.clone(),
    92	                            _ => expr.to_string(),
    93	                        };
    94	                        output.push((label.to_ascii_lowercase(), kind(expr, &sources)));
    95	                    }
    96	                    SelectItem::ExprWithAlias { expr, alias } => output.push((alias.value.to_ascii_lowercase(), kind(expr, &sources))),
    97	                    _ => return Err("unsupported object-row projection; use --json".into()),
    98	                }
    99	            }
   100	            let mut names = BTreeSet::new();
   101	            if output.iter().any(|(name, _)| !names.insert(name)) {
   102	                return Err("--json-objects needs unique names in every projection; alias duplicate columns".into());
   103	            }
   104	            Ok(output)
   105	        }
   106	        _ => Err("--json-objects needs a SELECT projection; use --json".into()),
   107	    }
   108	}
   109	
   110	fn kind(expr: &Expr, sources: &[(String, Columns)]) -> Kind {
   111	    let (table, column) = match expr {
   112	        Expr::Nested(expr) => return kind(expr, sources),
   113	        Expr::Identifier(id) => (None, &id.value),
   114	        Expr::CompoundIdentifier(ids) if ids.len() == 2 => (Some(ids[0].value.to_ascii_lowercase()), &ids[1].value),
   115	        _ => return Kind::Plain, // Computed SQL expressions retain SQLite's own types.
   116	    };
   117	    let found: Vec<Kind> = sources.iter().filter(|(alias, _)| table.as_ref().is_none_or(|t| t == alias))
   118	        .flat_map(|(_, cols)| cols.iter().filter(|(name, _)| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(column)).map(|(_, kind)| *kind)).collect();
   119	    // An unqualified duplicate only reaches execution when SQLite resolved it
   120	    // through USING/NATURAL. All contributors must agree before typing it bool.
   121	    found.first().copied().filter(|first| found.iter().all(|kind| kind == first)).unwrap_or(Kind::Plain)
   122	}

    24	async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {

    51	export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
    52	  personas: string[];
    53	  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
    54	  now(): Promise<number>;
    55	  advance(duration: string, options?: AdvanceOptions): Promise<ClockAdvance>;

    59	/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
    60	/** @throws invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. */
    61	export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
    62	  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
    63	  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
    64	  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
    65	  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
    66	  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
    67	  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
    68	  return host.then(host => {
    69	    const operatorToken = async (service = "operator", days = 1) => {
    70	      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 3650) throw Error("proof operatorToken: invalid service or days (1..3650)");
    71	      const port = new URL(host.url).port;
    72	      const result = await cli(["token", "--service", service, "--days", String(days), "--port", port, "--quiet"], opts);
    73	      if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`proof development operator token refused: ${result.stderr}`);
    74	      const token = result.stdout.trim();
    75	      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) throw Error("proof development operator token malformed");
    76	      return token;
    77	    };
    78	    const clockCall = async (body?: object): Promise<ClockAdvance> => {
    79	      const response = await fetch(`${host.url}/dev/clock/${body ? "advance" : "status"}`, { method: body ? "POST" : "GET", headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${await operatorToken()}`, "content-type": "application/json" }, ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 60_000)) });
    80	      const result = await response.json() as Partial<ClockAdvance> & {denied?:{code?:string;message?:string}};
    81	      if (!response.ok || result.denied || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.now)) throw Object.assign(Error(`development clock refused or reply unavailable; inspect before retrying: ${result.denied?.code ?? response.status} ${result.denied?.message ?? ""}`), {report:result});
    82	      const now = result.now!;
    83	      return { now, fired: result.fired ?? [], held: result.held ?? [], maintenancePaused: result.maintenancePaused ?? false, valueOf() { return now; } };
    84	    };
    85	    return { ...host, personas: host.personas!, operatorToken, now: async () => (await clockCall()).now,
    86	      advance: (duration: string, advanceOptions: AdvanceOptions = {}) => {
    87	        if (typeof duration !== "string" || (!/^\+?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(duration) || BigInt(duration.replace(/\D/g, "")) === 0n)) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: advance requires a positive whole duration");
    88	        return clockCall({duration, ...advanceOptions});
    89	      },
    90	      drain: (advanceOptions: Pick<AdvanceOptions,"eachBoundary"|"allowHeld"> = {}) => clockCall({duration:"0ms",drain:true,...advanceOptions}),
    91	    };
    92	  });
    93	}

   113	export interface AdvanceOptions { eachBoundary?: boolean; drain?: boolean; allowHeld?: boolean; sessions?: "preserve" | "expire" }
   114	export interface ServeHost extends ProofHost {
   115	  /** Authoritative staging time; requires a staging store and local operator authority. */
   116	  now(): Promise<number>;

   127	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — operator authority stays in the existing local store proof; bulk setup refuses production.
   128	/** Starts a release. stop() awaits native exit and kernel store release.
   129	 * @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. stop() rejects bounded cleanup failure. */
   130	export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
   131	  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
   132	  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
   133	  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
   134	  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && (typeof opts.clockOffset !== "string" || !/^[+-]?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(opts.clockOffset))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: clockOffset is a signed duration such as +25h");
   135	  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && opts.args?.some(arg => arg === "--clock-offset" || arg.startsWith("--clock-offset="))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: pass clockOffset once");
   136	  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], { ...opts, args: [...(opts.args ?? []), ...(opts.clockOffset === undefined ? [] : ["--clock-offset", opts.clockOffset])] });
   137	  const clockCall = async (body?: object): Promise<ClockAdvance> => {
   138	    const config = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/auth/config`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
   139	    if (!config.response.ok || config.body.environment !== "staging") throw Error("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY: clock controls require a deploy --staging store");
   140	    const clockToken = served.operatorToken("clock");
   141	    const response = await fetch(`${host.url}/dev/clock/${body ? "advance" : "status"}`, { method: body ? "POST" : "GET", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${await clockToken}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 60_000)) });
   142	    const result = await response.json() as Partial<ClockAdvance> & { sessionNotice?: string; denied?: { code?: string; message?: string } };
   143	    if (result.sessionNotice) process.stderr.write(`snapback4: ${result.sessionNotice}\n`);
   144	    if (!response.ok || result.denied || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.now)) throw Object.assign(Error(`staging clock refused or reply unavailable; inspect clock and queued work before retrying: ${result.denied?.code ?? response.status} ${result.denied?.message ?? ""}`), { report: result });
   145	    if (body && (!Array.isArray(result.fired) || !Array.isArray(result.held))) throw Error("staging clock reply has no schedule report; inspect clock and queued work before retrying");
   146	    for (const schedule of result.held ?? []) if (schedule.holdCode === "saved-boundary") process.stderr.write(`snapback4: schedule ${schedule.name}: ${schedule.reason}\n`);
   147	    const report: ClockAdvance = { now: result.now!, fired: result.fired ?? [], held: result.held ?? [], maintenancePaused: result.maintenancePaused ?? false, valueOf() { return this.now; } };
   148	    return report;
   372	export async function exhaustPages<Row>(readPage: (cursor: Cursor | null) => Promise<ProofPage<Row>>, options: ExhaustPagesOptions = {}): Promise<Row[]> {
   373	  const maxPages = options.maxPages ?? 1_000, label = options.label ?? "paged read";
   374	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxPages) || maxPages < 1) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: maxPages must be a positive safe integer`);
   375	  const rows: Row[] = [], seen = new Set<string>();
   376	  let cursor: Cursor | null = null;
   377	  for (let pageNumber = 1; pageNumber <= maxPages; pageNumber++) {
   378	    const page = await readPage(cursor);
   379	    if (!page || typeof page !== "object") throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned no read envelope`);
   380	    if ("denied" in page) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} refused ${page.denied.code}: ${page.denied.message}`);
   381	    if ("loading" in page) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is still loading`);
   382	    if (!Array.isArray(page.data)) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} data is not an array`);
   383	    if (page.cut !== null) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is incomplete (${page.cut === undefined ? "missing" : page.cut} cut)`);
   384	    if (page.complete !== true) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is incomplete (complete must be true)`);
   385	    if (page.existence === "partial") throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} has partial existence`);
   386	    rows.push(...page.data);
   387	    const next = page.next ?? null;
   388	    if (next === null) return rows;
   389	    if (typeof next !== "string" || next.length === 0) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned a malformed cursor`);
   390	    if (seen.has(next)) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned a repeated cursor`);
   391	    seen.add(next);
   392	    cursor = next as Cursor;
   393	  }
   394	  throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: exceeded maxPages ${maxPages}`);
   395	}

   397	/** @throws page closed or browser activation failed. */
   398	export async function frontPage(page: Page): Promise<Page> { await page.bringToFront(); return page; }

   215	        &mut self,

   223	            .unwrap_or(0);
   224	        if expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != held) {
   225	            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
   226	                "release revision changed; run releases and retry prune".into(),

   228	        }
   229	        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;

   232	            tx.rollback()?;
   233	            return Ok(

   235	            );
   236	        }
   237	        if !present(tx.conn())? {
   238	            tx.rollback()?;
   239	            return Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":[],"revision":held}));

   256	        let event_id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
   257	        super::backfills::prune(&tx)?;
   258	        tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _dormant_values;DELETE FROM _dormant_rows;DELETE FROM _dormant_refs;DELETE FROM _dormant_columns;DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes;DELETE FROM _dormant_tables;")?;
   259	        tx.commit()?;
   260	        Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":items,"warnings":warnings,"revision":revision,"event":event_id}))
   261	    }
   262	}

     1	//! Service-seat minting selects a project host, then proves local store authority.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	/// Mint a service token for the exact registered dev address selected by an
     5	/// operator command. The registry supplies the private store proof path; the
     6	/// host or a stopped store remains the only minting authority.
     7	pub(crate) fn mint_for_dev(address: std::net::SocketAddr, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<String, String> {
     8	    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
     9	    let project = Project::locate(&cwd).ok_or("cannot mint a development operator token outside a Snapback project")?;
    10	    let hosts = crate::dev_hosts::live(&project.root.canonicalize().unwrap_or(project.root.clone()))?;
    11	    let host = hosts.into_iter().find(|host| host.address == address)
    12	        .ok_or_else(|| format!("no registered dev host for this project at {address}; restart dev with this CLI"))?;
    13	    let path = host.store_path.ok_or("this older dev host does not publish its store proof path; restart it with this CLI")?;
    14	    let owner = match crate::store_owner::command(&path) {
    15	        Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
    16	        Err(error) if error.starts_with("E_STORE_BUSY ") => None,
    17	        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    18	    };
    19	    let session = if let Some(owner) = owner {
    20	        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| format!("cannot open the store: {e}"))?;
    21	        snapback4_core::identity::service_session(&store, name, snapback4_core::now_ms(), days).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
    22	    } else {
    23	        crate::serve::token::mint_live(&path, name, days).map_err(|(_, error)| error)?
    24	    };
    25	    Ok(session.token)
    26	}
