    28	/// `token --service <name> [--days N] [--release <dir>]`: a bearer session
    29	/// for a service principal, printed once. A service is a worker's seat: it
    30	/// claims effects, mints sessions for the accounts the application
    31	/// authenticates itself, and is admitted by rules as `service:<name>`.
    32	pub fn token(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    33	    let Some(name) = options.flags.get("service").filter(|name| !name.is_empty()) else {
    34	        eprintln!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("token"));
    35	        return 2;
    36	    };
    37	    let days: i64 = options.flags.get("days").and_then(|d| d.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(365);
    38	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — project registry selects; private mutual proof authorizes.
    39	    let seat_facts;
    40	    let path = match options.flags.get("release") {
    41	        Some(dir) => match releases::existing_deployment(Path::new(dir)) {
    42	            Ok(dir) => {
    43	                seat_facts = releases::bound_current(&dir).map(|bound| crate::service_identities::collect(&bound));
    44	                dir.join("store.sqlite")
    45	            },
    46	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("token: {error}"); return 2; }
    47	        },
    48	        None => {
    49	            let project = project_or_exit();
    50	            seat_facts = project.compile().map_err(|error| error.to_string()).and_then(|bound| if bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    51	                Ok(crate::service_identities::collect(&bound))
    52	            } else { Err("the working schema has compiler diagnostics".into()) });
    53	            let hosts = match crate::dev_hosts::live(&project.root.canonicalize().unwrap_or(project.root.clone())) {
    54	                Ok(hosts) => hosts,
    55	                Err(error) => { eprintln!("token: {error}"); return 2; }
    56	            };
    57	            let port = match options.flags.get("port") {
    58	                Some(value) => match value.parse::<u16>().ok().filter(|p| *p > 0) {
    59	                    Some(port) => Some(port),
    60	                    None => { eprintln!("E_INPUT: token --port must be an integer in 1..65535; no mint request sent"); return 2; }
    61	                },
    62	                None => None,
    63	            };
    64	            let hosts: Vec<_> = hosts.into_iter().filter(|h| port.is_none_or(|p| h.address.port() == p)).collect();
    65	            if hosts.len() > 1 { eprintln!("E_INPUT: multiple live dev hosts; select token --service {name} --port N"); return 2; }
    66	            if let Some(host) = hosts.first() {
    67	                match &host.store_path { Some(path) => path.clone(), None => { eprintln!("token: this older dev host does not publish its store proof path; restart it with this CLI"); return 2; } }
    68	            } else if port.is_some() {
    69	                eprintln!("E_INPUT: no registered dev host for this project on the selected port; start dev and use its printed port"); return 2;
    70	            } else { project.store_path() }
    71	        },
    72	    };
    73	    if !options.flags.contains_key("release") && !path.is_file() {
    74	        eprintln!("cannot open the store: no store at {}; run `snapback4 dev` first", path.display());
    75	        return 2;
    76	    }
    77	    let _owner = match crate::store_owner::command(&path) {
    78	        Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
    79	        Err(error) if error.starts_with("E_STORE_BUSY ") => None,
    80	        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 2; }
    81	    };
    82	    let store_path = &path;
    83	    let session = if let Some(owner) = &_owner {
    84	        let store = match owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)) {
    85	            Ok(store) => store,
    86	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("cannot open the store: {error}"); return 2; }
    87	        };
    88	        snapback4_core::identity::service_session(&store, name, snapback4_core::now_ms(), days)
    89	            .map_err(|error| (1, format!("token: {error}")))
    90	    } else {
    91	        crate::serve::token::mint_live(&path, name, days)
    92	    };
    93	    match session {
    94	        Ok(session) => {
    95	            if options.json {
    96	                println!("{}", json!({"token":session.token,"principal":session.principal,"expiresAt":session.expires_at,"store":store_path}));
    97	            } else {
    98	                println!("{}", session.token);
    99	                if !options.flags.contains_key("quiet") {
   100	                    eprintln!("token: a session for {} until {}; printed once; keep it where the worker keeps secrets", session.principal, session.expires_at);
   101	                    eprintln!("store: {}; this token belongs only to this store; each isolated store needs its own", store_path.display());
   102	                }
   103	            }
   104	            let principal = format!("service:{name}");
   105	            match &seat_facts {
   106	                Ok(seats) if !seats.contains(&principal) => {
   107	                    let known = if seats.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { seats.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") };
   108	                    eprintln!("token: WARNING minted {principal}, but the compiled schema reports no explicit positive authorization mention for that service identity; rules still decide every operation (explicit service identities: {known})");
   109	                }
   110	                Err(reason) => eprintln!("token: WARNING minted {principal}, but {reason}; verify an explicit positive authorization mention for this service identity because rules still decide every operation"),
   111	                _ => {}
   112	            }
   113	            0
   114	        }
   115	        Err((status, error)) => {
   116	            eprintln!("{error}");
   117	            status
   118	        }
   119	    }
   120	}

    46	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — reconcile the exact bearer before the SDK observes advanced business time.
    47	pub(super) fn session_clock_headers(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Vec<Header> {
    48	    if !shared.staging && !shared.personas { return clock_headers(shared).into(); }
    49	    let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(request) else { return clock_headers(shared).into(); };
    50	    // Detached readers never fall back to the writer: callers may still hold it.
    51	    // Keep the process clock fixed across the committed authority snapshot, so
    52	    // preserved lifetime and the clock stamp describe the same staging jump.
    53	    let Ok((epoch, reader)) = shared.reader() else {
    54	        return vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Timing", "unavailable").unwrap()];
    55	    };
    56	    let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
    57	    let clock = lock(&shared.clock);
    58	    let now = clock.now().max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire));
    59	    let timing = identity.as_deref().ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth("store identity unavailable".into()))
    60	        .and_then(|identity| reader.staging_session_timing(token, now, identity));
    61	    let headers = match timing {
    62	        Ok(timing) => {
    63	            let mut headers = vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Timing", "staging").unwrap(),
    64	                Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Time", now.to_string()).unwrap(),
    65	                Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Clock", clock.revision()).unwrap()];
    66	            if let Some(timing) = timing {
    67	                headers.push(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Expires", timing.expires_at.to_string()).unwrap());
    68	                if let Some(cap) = timing.cap_expires_at { headers.push(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Cap", cap.to_string()).unwrap()); }
    69	            }
    70	            headers
    71	        }
    72	        Err(_) => vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Timing", "unavailable").unwrap()],
    73	    };
    74	    drop(clock);
    75	    lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
    76	    headers
    77	}
    78	
    79	pub(crate) fn clock_headers(shared: &Shared) -> [Header; 2] {
    80	    let clock = lock(&shared.clock);
    81	    let now = clock.now().max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire));
    82	    [Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Time", now.to_string()).unwrap(),
    83	     Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Clock", clock.revision()).unwrap()]
    84	}

    87	// The online tier can discover /schema facts through its ordinary traffic.
    88	pub(crate) fn ephemeral_headers(shared: &Shared) -> Vec<Header> {
    89	    if lock(&shared.wake).eph.tables.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    90	    vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Ephemeral", "1").unwrap(),
    91	         Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Ephemeral").unwrap()]
    92	}

   790	// Prune forgets historical values, retaining only the fact that exact reversal
   791	// is no longer available. No tombstone can be queried by an app.
   792	pub(super) fn prune(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   793	    if present(tx)? {tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id); DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_pruned.t AND r.id=_backfill_pruned.id); INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_pruned(t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs) SELECT t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE (definition!='' AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%') OR NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_absence.t AND r.id=_backfill_absence.id);")?;}

**Fan-out can accept missing receipt-owned children after removal and pruning.** At frozen commit `e7e6a7fb370ea9888477139598e7780637a3bd43`, [extraction_each.rs:233](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b25-dx8/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/extraction_each.rs:233) silently continues when a removed element’s child is missing.

Concrete counterexample:

1. Extract v1 list `[A]` into v2 child C.
2. Return to v1; remove A through an authorized old-client edit.
3. Prune dormant history, deleting retained C while leaving its extraction receipt.
4. Return to v2. The populated-element loop checks nothing, and the stale-receipt loop skips missing C. Adoption succeeds.

This contradicts the amendment’s explicit missing/pruned-child refusal and retained-child custody. **Required correction:** validate every receipt-owned child and its parent-lifetime evidence before processing either present or removed elements. A valid retained child may receive the internal null; missing/pruned history must abort atomically. Add this combined removal → prune → return regression.

**Reverse fan-out also omits populated narrowed children.** Its membership index includes only active `r` rows ([extraction_each.rs:282](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b25-dx8/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/extraction_each.rs:282)). Narrow a child through an unrelated optional text field while leaving its asset type unchanged, then select v1: that child’s asset is omitted from the reconstructed list. **Required correction:** reconcile retained populated children as well, distinguishing them from migration-owned null removals; gather the full population or refuse explicitly.

The capability/store fences and the three stated round-one repairs are present. These concerns arise from source inspection, independently of pending verification.
